Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the biochemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 542. The moduli space has dimension at most 574 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to biochemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 542 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 574. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
